 SCRIPT NUMBER 15, project:AGBT26A_644_17 
 323007 128 OPERATOR 12 Rob Taggart 2026-11-09 07:50:02 obs_completed 
#######################################################
# Script name: 26A644_operator_scienceonly
# This script skips pointing and flux calibrations
# Y.-H. Eltha Teng, Oct 2026

########## SETTINGS #############
h = Horizon(18.0)  # minimum of 18 degrees elevation
myOff = Offset("AzEl", 1.0, 0.0, cosv=True)  # Off position relative to source
doFirstPointing = False  # Setting to False will skip the first pointing
doFirstCal = False  # Setting to False will skip the first 3C calibrator OnOff scan
scanTime = 180  # 180 seconds = 3 min for an on OR off scan 
n_scans = 5  # do OnOff for 5 time on each science source (i.e. 15 min on + 15 min off per source)
n_repeat = 1  # observe once per source
keyhole = 76  # maximum elevation limit of GBT (in degree)
#####################################

# In case configuration changed:

ResetConfig()

config_string = """
receiver = 'Rcvr1_2'
obstype = 'Spectroscopy'
backend = 'VEGAS'
restfreq = 1420.4058
bandwidth = 187.5
nchan = 32768
swmode = "tp"
swtype = "none"
swper = 1.0
tint = 5.0
vframe = "bary"
vdef = "Optical"
noisecal = "lo"
pol = "Linear"
"""

Configure(config_string)


# Skipped pointings and fluxcals

execfile("/home/astro-util/projects/26A644/26A644_functions.py")
Catalog(fluxcal)
Catalog(lband_pointing)

tdelta = DateTime.TimeDelta(minutes=5)  
obs_count = 0
doCal = doFirstCal
doPointing = doFirstPointing
project_obs = 0
old_catalog_name = 'dummy'
fullcat = '/home/astro-util/projects/26A644/targets.cat'


while project_obs < 100:
    
    mycat, altcal = select_my_catalog()
    obs_per_source = n_repeat * n_scans
    
    if mycat == old_catalog_name:
        print 'SAME CATALOG HAS BEEN SELECTED: Using FULL catalog'
        mycat = fullcat
        altcal = '3C309_1'    
    old_catalog_name = mycat

    c = Catalog(mycat)
    sources = c.keys()

    for source in sources:
        
        doBalance = True
        
        ## Pick an observable science source along with its nearby calibrator
        calibrator = c[source]["calibrator"]
        n_obs = int(float(c[source]["n_obs"]))
        obs_left = obs_per_source - n_obs
        obs_ok = obs_left > 0

        t_now = Now()
        alt_ok = True  # source altitute should be ok in most cases 
        if t_now != None and obs_ok:
            source_rise = h.GetRise(source)
            source_set  = h.GetSet(source)
            if source_rise != None:
                if source_set != None:
                    t_ok = ((t_now + tdelta) < source_set)
                    t_ok = t_ok and (t_now >= source_rise)
                else:
                    t_ok = True
            else:
                t_ok = False
            
            if t_ok:
                ## Skip source if it is near zenith
                source_ra = c[source]["ra"]  # float in deg
                source_dec = c[source]["dec"]  # float in deg
                source_alt = get_altitude(source_ra, source_dec, t_now)
                alt_ok = False if source_alt > keyhole else True
        else:
            t_ok = False

        source_ok = t_ok and obs_ok and alt_ok

        ## Source confirmed; calibrate (if first source) and do science scans
        if source_ok:
            if doPointing == True:
                ## check the calibrator is above the horizon
                if h.GetRise(calibrator) > t_now:
                    myCal = altcal ##circumpolar alternative
                    print 'Calibrator %s is too low: Using %s' %(calibrator,altcal)
                else:
                    myCal = calibrator
                if obs_count == 0:
                    AutoPeakFocus(myCal)
                    #Break("Check Peak and Focus")
                else:
                    AutoPeak(myCal)

                ResetConfig()
                Configure(config_string)
                doBalance = True
                doPointing = False

            if doCal:
                Slew(myCal)
                Balance()
                print 'Calibration scans (OnOff) :%s ' %(myCal)
                OnOff(myCal, myOff, scanTime)
                doCal = False
                doBalance = True

            ## Starting science scans
            for i in range(obs_left):
                ## Checking time ok again
                t_now = Now()   
                t_ok = False
                if t_now != None:
                    if source_rise != None:
                        if source_set != None:
                            t_ok = ((t_now + tdelta) < source_set)
                            t_ok = t_ok and (t_now >= source_rise)
                        else:
                            t_ok = True
                    else:
                        t_ok = False
                else:
                    t_ok = False

                ## Starting science OnOff scans
                if t_ok:
                    ## Re-balance when source changes
                    if doBalance:
                        Slew(source)
                        Balance()
                        doBalance = False

                    Comment("Starting on-off scan")
                    OnOff(source, myOff, scanTime)

                    obs_count = obs_count + 1
                    obs_tot = obs_per_source + 1 - obs_left + i
                    print '%s: OBS COMPLETE = %s/%s' %(source, obs_tot, obs_per_source)
                    
                    update_my_catalogs(source, obs_tot)

        if source_ok and mycat == fullcat:
            break
    
    project_obs = project_obs + 1

doFirstPointing = True  # Setting to False will skip the first pointing
doFirstCal = True  # Setting to False will skip the first 3C calibrator OnOff scan
scanTime = 180  # 180 seconds = 3 min for an on OR off scan 
n_scans = 5  # do OnOff for 5 time on each science source (i.e. 15 min on + 15 min off per source)
n_repeat = 1  # observe once per source
keyhole = 76  # maximum elevation limit of GBT (in degree)
#####################################

ResetConfig()

config_string = """
receiver = 'Rcvr1_2'
obstype = 'Spectroscopy'
backend = 'VEGAS'
restfreq = 1420.4058
bandwidth = 187.5
nchan = 32768
swmode = "tp"
swtype = "none"
swper = 1.0
tint = 5.0
vframe = "bary"
vdef = "Optical"
noisecal = "lo"
pol = "Linear"
"""

Configure(config_string)

execfile("/home/astro-util/projects/26A644/26A644_functions.py")
Catalog(fluxcal)
Catalog(lband_pointing)

tdelta = DateTime.TimeDelta(minutes=5)  
obs_count = 0
doCal = doFirstCal
doPointing = doFirstPointing
project_obs = 0
old_catalog_name = 'dummy'
fullcat = '/home/astro-util/projects/26A644/targets.cat'


while project_obs < 100:
    
    mycat, altcal = select_my_catalog()
    obs_per_source = n_repeat * n_scans
    
    if mycat == old_catalog_name:
        print 'SAME CATALOG HAS BEEN SELECTED: Using FULL catalog'
        mycat = fullcat
        altcal = '3C309_1'    
    old_catalog_name = mycat

    c = Catalog(mycat)
    sources = c.keys()

    for source in sources:
        
        doBalance = True
        
        ## Pick an observable science source along with its nearby calibrator
        calibrator = c[source]["calibrator"]
        n_obs = int(float(c[source]["n_obs"]))
        obs_left = obs_per_source - n_obs
        obs_ok = obs_left > 0

        t_now = Now()
        alt_ok = True  # source altitute should be ok in most cases 
        if t_now != None and obs_ok:
            source_rise = h.GetRise(source)
            source_set  = h.GetSet(source)
            if source_rise != None:
                if source_set != None:
                    t_ok = ((t_now + tdelta) < source_set)
                    t_ok = t_ok and (t_now >= source_rise)
                else:
                    t_ok = True
            else:
                t_ok = False
            
            if t_ok:
                ## Skip source if it is near zenith
                source_ra = c[source]["ra"]  # float in deg
                source_dec = c[source]["dec"]  # float in deg
                source_alt = get_altitude(source_ra, source_dec, t_now)
                alt_ok = False if source_alt > keyhole else True
        else:
            t_ok = False

        source_ok = t_ok and obs_ok and alt_ok

        ## Source confirmed; calibrate (if first source) and do science scans
        if source_ok:
            if doPointing == True:
                ## check the calibrator is above the horizon
                if h.GetRise(calibrator) > t_now:
                    myCal = altcal ##circumpolar alternative
                    print 'Calibrator %s is too low: Using %s' %(calibrator,altcal)
                else:
                    myCal = calibrator
                if obs_count == 0:
                    AutoPeakFocus(myCal)
                    #Break("Check Peak and Focus")
                else:
                    AutoPeak(myCal)

                ResetConfig()
                Configure(config_string)
                doBalance = True
                doPointing = False

            if doCal:
                Slew(myCal)
                Balance()
                print 'Calibration scans (OnOff) :%s ' %(myCal)
                OnOff(myCal, myOff, scanTime)
                doCal = False
                doBalance = True

            ## Starting science scans
            for i in range(obs_left):
                ## Checking time ok again
                t_now = Now()   
                t_ok = False
                if t_now != None:
                    if source_rise != None:
                        if source_set != None:
                            t_ok = ((t_now + tdelta) < source_set)
                            t_ok = t_ok and (t_now >= source_rise)
                        else:
                            t_ok = True
                    else:
                        t_ok = False
                else:
                    t_ok = False

                ## Starting science OnOff scans
                if t_ok:
                    ## Re-balance when source changes
                    if doBalance:
                        Slew(source)
                        Balance()
                        doBalance = False

                    Comment("Starting on-off scan")
                    OnOff(source, myOff, scanTime)

                    obs_count = obs_count + 1
                    obs_tot = obs_per_source + 1 - obs_left + i
                    print '%s: OBS COMPLETE = %s/%s' %(source, obs_tot, obs_per_source)
                    
                    update_my_catalogs(source, obs_tot)

        if source_ok and mycat == fullcat:
            break
    
    project_obs = project_obs + 1

#######################################################

#######################################################
 SCRIPT NUMBER 17, project:AGBT26A_644_17 
 323009 128 OPERATOR 12 Rob Taggart 2026-11-09 08:02:28 obs_completed 
#######################################################
# Script name: 26A644_operator_backup
# This backup script searches for science target from the full catalog
# Y.-H. Eltha Teng, Oct 2026 

from mx import DateTime

########## SETTINGS #############
h = Horizon(18.0)  # minimum of 18 degrees elevation
myOff = Offset("AzEl", 1.0, 0.0, cosv=True)  # Off position relative to source
doFirstPointing = True  # Setting to False will skip the first pointing
doFirstCal = True   # Setting to False will skip the first 3C calibrator OnOff scan
scanTime = 180  # 180 seconds = 3 min for an on OR off scan 
n_scans = 5  #do OnOff for 5 time on each science source (i.e. 15 min on + 15 min off per source)
#####################################


config_string = """
receiver = 'Rcvr1_2'
obstype = 'Spectroscopy'
backend = 'VEGAS'
restfreq = 1420.4058
bandwidth = 187.5
nchan = 32768
swmode = "tp"
swtype = "none"
swper = 1.0
tint = 5.0
vframe = "bary"
vdef = "Optical"
noisecal = "lo"
pol = "Linear"
"""

Catalog(fluxcal)
Catalog(lband_pointing)


LST = GetLST()
if LST >= 5 and LST <= 9:
    altcal = "3C249_1"
elif LST > 9 and LST <= 13:
    altcal = "3C249_1"
elif LST > 13 and LST <= 17:
    altcal = "3C309_1"
elif LST > 17 or LST < 5: #and LST <= 21:   #21 nominally
    altcal = "3C309_1"
c = Catalog("/home/astro-util/projects/26A644/targets.cat")

sources = c.keys()
tdelta = DateTime.TimeDelta(minutes=5)  
obs_count = 0
doCal = doFirstCal
doPointing = doFirstPointing
obs_per_source = n_scans


for source in sources:
    
    doBalance = True
    
    ## Pick an observable science source along with its nearby calibrator
    calibrator = c[source]["calibrator"]
    n_obs = int(float(c[source]["n_obs"]))
    obs_left = obs_per_source - n_obs
    obs_ok = obs_left > 0

    t_now = Now()
    if t_now != None and obs_ok:
        source_rise = h.GetRise(source)
        source_set  = h.GetSet(source)
        if source_rise != None:
            if source_set != None:
                t_ok = ((t_now + tdelta) < source_set)
                t_ok = t_ok and (t_now >= source_rise)
            else:
                t_ok = True
        else:
            t_ok = False
    else:
        t_ok = False

    source_ok = t_ok and obs_ok

    ## Source confirmed; calibrate (if first source) and do science scans
    if source_ok:
        if doPointing == True:
            ## check the calibrator is above the horizon
            if h.GetRise(calibrator) > t_now:
                myCal = altcal ##circumpolar alternative
                print 'Calibrator %s is too low: Using %s' %(calibrator,altcal)
            else:
                myCal = calibrator
            if obs_count == 0:
                AutoPeakFocus(myCal)
                #Break("Check Peak and Focus")
            else:
                AutoPeak(myCal)

            ResetConfig()
            Configure(config_string)
            doBalance = True
            doPointing = False

        if doCal:
            Slew(myCal)
            Balance()
            print 'Calibration scans (OnOff) :%s ' %(myCal)
            OnOff(myCal, myOff, scanTime)
            doCal = False
            doBalance = True

        ## Starting science scans
        for i in range(obs_left):
            ## Checking time ok again
            t_now = Now()   
            t_ok = False
            if t_now != None:
                if source_rise != None:
                    if source_set != None:
                        t_ok = ((t_now + tdelta) < source_set)
                        t_ok = t_ok and (t_now >= source_rise)
                    else:
                        t_ok = True
                else:
                    t_ok = False
            else:
                t_ok = False

            ## Starting science OnOff scans
            if t_ok:
                ## Need to re-Balance/re-Peak after source changes?
                # if peak_near_source and newPeak:
                #     Slew(source)
                #     AutoPeak()
                #     Configure(config_string)
                #     newPeak = False
                #     doBalance = True
                if doBalance:
                    Slew(source)
                    Balance()
                    doBalance = False

                Comment("Starting on-off scan")
                OnOff(source, myOff, scanTime)

                obs_count = obs_count + 1
                obs_tot = obs_per_source + 1 - obs_left + i
                print '%s: OBS COMPLETE = %s/%s' %(source, obs_tot, obs_per_source)
                
                update_my_catalogs(source, obs_tot)
                
                ## Do we need to re-peak after some time ? 
                # if obs_count % peak_every == 0:  #Cal/pointing interval
                #     doCal = True
                #     doPointing = True

 SCRIPT NUMBER 18, project:AGBT26A_644_17 
 323010 128 OPERATOR 12 Rob Taggart 2026-11-09 08:02:36 obs_completed 
#######################################################
# Script name: 26A644_operator_scienceonly
# This script skips pointing and flux calibrations
# Y.-H. Eltha Teng, Oct 2026

########## SETTINGS #############
h = Horizon(18.0)  # minimum of 18 degrees elevation
myOff = Offset("AzEl", 1.0, 0.0, cosv=True)  # Off position relative to source
doFirstPointing = False  # Setting to False will skip the first pointing
doFirstCal = False  # Setting to False will skip the first 3C calibrator OnOff scan
scanTime = 180  # 180 seconds = 3 min for an on OR off scan 
n_scans = 5  # do OnOff for 5 time on each science source (i.e. 15 min on + 15 min off per source)
n_repeat = 1  # observe once per source
keyhole = 76  # maximum elevation limit of GBT (in degree)
#####################################

# In case configuration changed:

ResetConfig()

config_string = """
receiver = 'Rcvr1_2'
obstype = 'Spectroscopy'
backend = 'VEGAS'
restfreq = 1420.4058
bandwidth = 187.5
nchan = 32768
swmode = "tp"
swtype = "none"
swper = 1.0
tint = 5.0
vframe = "bary"
vdef = "Optical"
noisecal = "lo"
pol = "Linear"
"""

Configure(config_string)


# Skipped pointings and fluxcals

execfile("/home/astro-util/projects/26A644/26A644_functions.py")
Catalog(fluxcal)
Catalog(lband_pointing)

tdelta = DateTime.TimeDelta(minutes=5)  
obs_count = 0
doCal = doFirstCal
doPointing = doFirstPointing
project_obs = 0
old_catalog_name = 'dummy'
fullcat = '/home/astro-util/projects/26A644/targets.cat'


while project_obs < 100:
    
    mycat, altcal = select_my_catalog()
    obs_per_source = n_repeat * n_scans
    
    if mycat == old_catalog_name:
        print 'SAME CATALOG HAS BEEN SELECTED: Using FULL catalog'
        mycat = fullcat
        altcal = '3C309_1'    
    old_catalog_name = mycat

    c = Catalog(mycat)
    sources = c.keys()

    for source in sources:
        
        doBalance = True
        
        ## Pick an observable science source along with its nearby calibrator
        calibrator = c[source]["calibrator"]
        n_obs = int(float(c[source]["n_obs"]))
        obs_left = obs_per_source - n_obs
        obs_ok = obs_left > 0

        t_now = Now()
        alt_ok = True  # source altitute should be ok in most cases 
        if t_now != None and obs_ok:
            source_rise = h.GetRise(source)
            source_set  = h.GetSet(source)
            if source_rise != None:
                if source_set != None:
                    t_ok = ((t_now + tdelta) < source_set)
                    t_ok = t_ok and (t_now >= source_rise)
                else:
                    t_ok = True
            else:
                t_ok = False
            
            if t_ok:
                ## Skip source if it is near zenith
                source_ra = c[source]["ra"]  # float in deg
                source_dec = c[source]["dec"]  # float in deg
                source_alt = get_altitude(source_ra, source_dec, t_now)
                alt_ok = False if source_alt > keyhole else True
        else:
            t_ok = False

        source_ok = t_ok and obs_ok and alt_ok

        ## Source confirmed; calibrate (if first source) and do science scans
        if source_ok:
            if doPointing == True:
                ## check the calibrator is above the horizon
                if h.GetRise(calibrator) > t_now:
                    myCal = altcal ##circumpolar alternative
                    print 'Calibrator %s is too low: Using %s' %(calibrator,altcal)
                else:
                    myCal = calibrator
                if obs_count == 0:
                    AutoPeakFocus(myCal)
                    #Break("Check Peak and Focus")
                else:
                    AutoPeak(myCal)

                ResetConfig()
                Configure(config_string)
                doBalance = True
                doPointing = False

            if doCal:
                Slew(myCal)
                Balance()
                print 'Calibration scans (OnOff) :%s ' %(myCal)
                OnOff(myCal, myOff, scanTime)
                doCal = False
                doBalance = True

            ## Starting science scans
            for i in range(obs_left):
                ## Checking time ok again
                t_now = Now()   
                t_ok = False
                if t_now != None:
                    if source_rise != None:
                        if source_set != None:
                            t_ok = ((t_now + tdelta) < source_set)
                            t_ok = t_ok and (t_now >= source_rise)
                        else:
                            t_ok = True
                    else:
                        t_ok = False
                else:
                    t_ok = False

                ## Starting science OnOff scans
                if t_ok:
                    ## Re-balance when source changes
                    if doBalance:
                        Slew(source)
                        Balance()
                        doBalance = False

                    Comment("Starting on-off scan")
                    OnOff(source, myOff, scanTime)

                    obs_count = obs_count + 1
                    obs_tot = obs_per_source + 1 - obs_left + i
                    print '%s: OBS COMPLETE = %s/%s' %(source, obs_tot, obs_per_source)
                    
                    update_my_catalogs(source, obs_tot)

        if source_ok and mycat == fullcat:
            break
    
    project_obs = project_obs + 1

doFirstCal = True  # Setting to False will skip the first 3C calibrator OnOff scan
scanTime = 180  # 180 seconds = 3 min for an on OR off scan 
n_scans = 5  # do OnOff for 5 time on each science source (i.e. 15 min on + 15 min off per source)
n_repeat = 1  # observe once per source
keyhole = 76  # maximum elevation limit of GBT (in degree)
#####################################

ResetConfig()

config_string = """
receiver = 'Rcvr1_2'
obstype = 'Spectroscopy'
backend = 'VEGAS'
restfreq = 1420.4058
bandwidth = 187.5
nchan = 32768
swmode = "tp"
swtype = "none"
swper = 1.0
tint = 5.0
vframe = "bary"
vdef = "Optical"
noisecal = "lo"
pol = "Linear"
"""

Configure(config_string)

execfile("/home/astro-util/projects/26A644/26A644_functions.py")
Catalog(fluxcal)
Catalog(lband_pointing)

tdelta = DateTime.TimeDelta(minutes=5)  
obs_count = 0
doCal = doFirstCal
doPointing = doFirstPointing
project_obs = 0
old_catalog_name = 'dummy'
fullcat = '/home/astro-util/projects/26A644/targets.cat'


while project_obs < 100:
    
    mycat, altcal = select_my_catalog()
    obs_per_source = n_repeat * n_scans
    
    if mycat == old_catalog_name:
        print 'SAME CATALOG HAS BEEN SELECTED: Using FULL catalog'
        mycat = fullcat
        altcal = '3C309_1'    
    old_catalog_name = mycat

    c = Catalog(mycat)
    sources = c.keys()

    for source in sources:
        
        doBalance = True
        
        ## Pick an observable science source along with its nearby calibrator
        calibrator = c[source]["calibrator"]
        n_obs = int(float(c[source]["n_obs"]))
        obs_left = obs_per_source - n_obs
        obs_ok = obs_left > 0

        t_now = Now()
        alt_ok = True  # source altitute should be ok in most cases 
        if t_now != None and obs_ok:
            source_rise = h.GetRise(source)
            source_set  = h.GetSet(source)
            if source_rise != None:
                if source_set != None:
                    t_ok = ((t_now + tdelta) < source_set)
                    t_ok = t_ok and (t_now >= source_rise)
                else:
                    t_ok = True
            else:
                t_ok = False
            
            if t_ok:
                ## Skip source if it is near zenith
                source_ra = c[source]["ra"]  # float in deg
                source_dec = c[source]["dec"]  # float in deg
                source_alt = get_altitude(source_ra, source_dec, t_now)
                alt_ok = False if source_alt > keyhole else True
        else:
            t_ok = False

        source_ok = t_ok and obs_ok and alt_ok

        ## Source confirmed; calibrate (if first source) and do science scans
        if source_ok:
            if doPointing == True:
                ## check the calibrator is above the horizon
                if h.GetRise(calibrator) > t_now:
                    myCal = altcal ##circumpolar alternative
                    print 'Calibrator %s is too low: Using %s' %(calibrator,altcal)
                else:
                    myCal = calibrator
                if obs_count == 0:
                    AutoPeakFocus(myCal)
                    #Break("Check Peak and Focus")
                else:
                    AutoPeak(myCal)

                ResetConfig()
                Configure(config_string)
                doBalance = True
                doPointing = False

            if doCal:
                Slew(myCal)
                Balance()
                print 'Calibration scans (OnOff) :%s ' %(myCal)
                OnOff(myCal, myOff, scanTime)
                doCal = False
                doBalance = True

            ## Starting science scans
            for i in range(obs_left):
                ## Checking time ok again
                t_now = Now()   
                t_ok = False
                if t_now != None:
                    if source_rise != None:
                        if source_set != None:
                            t_ok = ((t_now + tdelta) < source_set)
                            t_ok = t_ok and (t_now >= source_rise)
                        else:
                            t_ok = True
                    else:
                        t_ok = False
                else:
                    t_ok = False

                ## Starting science OnOff scans
                if t_ok:
                    ## Re-balance when source changes
                    if doBalance:
                        Slew(source)
                        Balance()
                        doBalance = False

                    Comment("Starting on-off scan")
                    OnOff(source, myOff, scanTime)

                    obs_count = obs_count + 1
                    obs_tot = obs_per_source + 1 - obs_left + i
                    print '%s: OBS COMPLETE = %s/%s' %(source, obs_tot, obs_per_source)
                    
                    update_my_catalogs(source, obs_tot)

        if source_ok and mycat == fullcat:
            break
    
    project_obs = project_obs + 1

#######################################################
